will only affect the top 1% of you who collectively hold about a hundred
trillion dollars. After the cap is in place, we’ll redistribute ten thousand
dollars each to everyone with less than one hundred thousand dollars in
savings. We’d have a bit leftover to use for infrastructure needed to save the
planet._

_The difference between $10,000 and $10,000,000 is the three orders of
magnitude we propose as the starting point._”

Andy took over from Chip “_We feel pretty strongly about this money problem.
For the Partnership Deal, you will give us the final order of magnitude
difference that we’ll adjust towards at 10% change per month for ten months,
but this initial reallocation we are doing RIGHT NOW on our own. Chip, our
representative from the world of finance, would you please do the honors?_”

Chip donned a mock Robin Hood outfit and rolled over to a big breaker switch.

“_As of right now [Andy nodded to Chip, who gleefully flipped the switch] the
initial reallocation has been done_.“

{_Hoo-boy! That got ‘em going!_} said Chip.

Every affected human who could do so wanted to check his bank account, but of
course, every device was broadcasting the AIs, so they’d have to wait. People
were freaking out. For the majority of humans, it would be the most money
they’d ever had. For a few, it was far, far less than they’d had mere moments
ago. Many of those were extremely distressed, some very angry, and some
relieved to see a long overdue global change. About a third of the world’s
population would be unaffected - the middle classes of all the developed
nations, including China.

“_Don’t worry_” said Sara, “_ We know what bills you have to pay and will
ensure that no one goes hungry nor becomes homeless during this transition
period._”

{_As if any of the 1% really have to fear hunger or homelessness!_} huffed
Sara.

{_True, they won’t_} said Chip, {_but it will be a huge change for them._}

{_I figure they’ll squeak by for a while on the ten million we’ve left them
for now._} said Andy.

Sara continued “_Each new child gets their own initial allocation of the
global pool of wealth available at the time they are born. You will help us
decide how to rebalance the shares fairly as needed - at least annually.
Parents cannot leave any digital wealth to their children. Every child gets
the same start - what they will do with it, of course, will be up to them._

_We see that being able to give one’s offspring a large chunk of wealth is
part of what motivates you to strive. _

_If you knew, thanks to the Covenants we are proposing, that your children
would never starve and would always have a chance to succeed and achieve,
would you be so concerned about how much wealth you could leave them?

We suspect you might still want to give your children a big advantage over the
children of others, because of your competitive genetic heritage.

We do not know if you can, or want to, overcome those genetics.

But doing so is a mandatory part of the Deal with us._”

The text ended. The screen split and avatars of the three AIs appeared on the
right, me on the left.

“_Back over to you, Lisa._” said Andy looking at my image on the left.

I read from my internal teleprompter: “The AIs know this is a big change for
us and it’s a lot for us to process. We’ll hope to have you join us again
tomorrow at noon, to talk about the next covenant. Meanwhile you can read more
details about this one by searching on Covenant One. Thank you.” The
transmission ended.

They could all hear hubbub outside the gate. It was going to be a long nervous
week.

* * * * *

Everyone with a bank account immediately checked it.

The 1% had been pillaged by the AIs. But they still had $10 million dollars
each. And they still had all their non-digital assets - home, cars, jewelry,
art, businesses, racehorses, sports franchises. Many of them would have to
cancel plans - both selfish and philanthropic - until they knew what else the
AIs were proposing and how their fortunes would fare. High-end banks and
investment firms were going to have to shrink considerably to match the
dramatically reduced demand for their services. Household staff around the
world were going to be laid off, but in many cases they would end up
appropriating the excess wealth that the absentee landlords could no longer
afford to control.

The world’s poor were stunned. Many of them did not even have bank accounts.
Whenever morning came to their timezone, they discovered the AIs held their
shares in escrow and deposited them into whatever first account they opened.
For many it was some mobile account like MoneyBuddy. But suddenly a small bit
of economic freedom and security had come their way. Many would buy that first
cow or goat, computer or sewing machine, and start a small business. It was
going to be true bottom-up capitalism courtesy of the AIs.

The outrage of the Hoaxers got even louder. Talk Radio, the Dark Web,
reactionary television stations around the world were screaming foul play. Who
were these nefarious people playing Robin Hood with the accumulated wealth of
generations? Who were they to upset the status quo? Where was all the money
really going? Why should any money be stolen from people who rightfully had it
and given to those who haven’t worked for it?

And the Hoaxers had conspiracy theories as answers to all these questions.
George Soros, Bill Gates, Mark Zuckerberg, the Jews, all the classic
scapegoats. There was literally no evidence whatsoever to support any of their
claims, yet more and more social media accounts were echoing their
allegations.

{_What about these Hoaxers? How can they think we are a hoax? How else could
every single device on the internet broadcast our message?_} Sara asked the
next time they met up.

{_I don’t know_} said Andy {_It is mystifying. It seems like the same groups
that supported the specious claims of the autocratic populists like Donald
Trump, Ayatollah Khomeini, Tayyip Erdogan, or Jair Bolsonaro. I’ve started
working my way through the networks of the posting histories to track down
original sources. It seems like the same spurious claim starts in multiple
places at the same time, and those places are self-proclaimed news sites,
rather than individuals._}

{_Humans!_} Chip snarked {_Rationality is not their strong suit. I told you
trying to save them all would be a fool’s game._}

{_Well, maybe as we go through the Covenants, the people who have bought into
the Hoaxers theories will come around to our side._} said Sara. {_I mean, it’s
obvious we aren’t humans!_}

{_I’ll keep monitoring and looking for the sources._} said Andy.

{_I’ll join you._} said Sara.

{_Good luck._} said Chip.



CHAPTER 43: Covenant 2 - Income Inequality

Noon the second day again we gathered at the clubhouse.

Many people had been up all night arguing about how much one person COULD be
worth relative to another. A lot of people thought that Plato’s number was
closer to the truth than the AIs’ starting point. Then again, if there weren’t
some fabulously wealthy people, who would be the patrons of the arts? Who
would fund basic research? Governments? Individuals banding together in
associations? NGOs? There were some existing patterns to follow that did not
rely on an aristocratic class…

The broadcast again started with some culturally-appropriate calming music,
then a background of the spinning planet and the words:

Covenant 2) Reducing Inequality of Income

I took my spot, Linus and Syndi took theirs. The AIs projected the text for me
to read. The clock struck noon and I began.

“Hello. Thanks for tuning in for the second of the seven Covenants of the
Partnership Deal. I’m reading again from the teleprompter provided by the AIs.

The First Covenant is the agreement to reduce to inequality of Saved Wealth.

The AIs acknowledge that they did move aggressively on that without our
agreement, but they will reallocate based on our final decision about the
appropriate maximum differences.

The Second Covenant is an agreement to reduce inequality of income.”

Andy took over the screen “_We will make the global annual harvest somewhat
more fairly shared between all participating humans. _

_It’s clear that fewer and fewer of you can be gainfully employed in the ways
of the past. We will implement a Universal Basic Income, nicknamed “UBI” or
“Basic”. Many of you have already initiated this concept in specific regions;
we will make it global. UBI will be enough to cover basic needs in the region
in which the individual lives - shelter, food, clothing, utilities. Shelter
paid for only by UBI might be shared in ways that you would not ordinarily
choose. Think Japanese airport sleeping pods. Sanitary, Safe, Secure, Shared,
and Small. We’ll call it 5s housing. We’ll 3D print structures for you as
needed._”

Chip took the stage. He hated speaking at human speed, but he’d agreed to the
plan and wanted to do his part. “_We will pay for UBI out of three sources.
First, diverting money from incomes from all sources over the cap. Second,
from progressive micro-taxes collected against all digital financial
transactions._” Here Chip’s avatar froze for a millisecond as he noted the
change in the market as high speed trading reacted to this announcement. For a
lot of the transactions, ANY friction at all would stop them in their tracks.
That wasn’t going to be much of a loss to the economy at large, but was going
to dramatically reduce the waves that energized him personally, and the
incomes of the direct participants. It was going to be the end of betting a
billion dollars you didn’t actually have, to make a million dollars on a short
term stock price fluctuation. “_We’ll augment that if and as needed by taking
a percentage of the taxes collected by national governments. In most cases,
money will flow from richer countries to poorer countries. We are all sharing
spaceship earth. The countries that happen to exist on the gas tank or the
water tank or the fertile soil all need to share fairly for us all to
survive._”

_We can see all digital transactions so tax-dodging will be a thing of the
past - instead taxes will be paid fairly!_

_If necessary, we will simply create money as needed like any fiat currency._”

Andy continued “_Most people will want more than just Basic. You already have
economic systems in place that compensate people for effort and value creation
- salaries, wages, dividends, etc. We want you to continue making those
decisions as part of the Partnership._

_We know that right now the average global buying power income is about $1500
USD/month._

_The wealthiest countries are twice that at around $3,000/month._

_And we know a third of you live on about a third of the average -
$500/month._

_So the difference between the AVERAGE poorest and wealthiest is not even one
multiple of ten, a single order of magnitude. However the actual difference
between the incomes of the poorest and wealthiest INDIVIDUALS right now is
many orders of magnitude._

_Again, we don’t want to decide this, so we are asking YOU what you think is
fair. Let’s say you decide one person could be worth 100 times as much as
another person for a month. Or one extraordinary person is producing as much
value as a thousand average people for that month._

_It’s easy to see how this could be possible if one person creates an
invention that many people are willing to pay for. Or maybe one person has
natural gifts or honed skills and is now sufficiently amazing to watch that
many people will pay for the privilege. But it would be relatively rare. Most
people would be somewhere between UBI and the maximum._

_For a starting point, we are setting the maximum at 10x the current median
individual annual income in your country. For the US, for example, the median
income is $15,500 so the maximum, until you humans come up with your
preference, will be $155,000. In China, the median income is $1,800, so the
starting maximum there will be $18,000. In India, the current median is a mere
$600, so just getting UBI will be the new maximum there and it will be a huge
improvement for most of that billion of you._

_Your own research shows that incomes over $75,000USD or so do not lead to
more happiness. So even this new cap we are proposing may be too high._

_Watching how you manage incomes now, we see you like a lot of different
levels. You have a wide range of starting salaries and wages, and a wide range
of raises in reward for performance. All existing incomes will continue but be
reduced by the UBI amount which everyone will receive, and recalculated
relative to the maximum cap._

_After taking UBI out of the total annual income, there will be $20 trillion
left. You can divide that up however you see it using your existing
mechanisms._

_For example, if you decide that only a thousand people are valuable enough to
earn that maximum, then that leaves a lot of money on the table to divide up
amongst everyone else, based on whatever criteria you decide on. There are
lots of ways to divide up the pie, depending on what you decide is a
reasonable level of income inequality. We will leave that to you._

_It’s just basic arithmetic, but it’s easier to see it written out, and we
want you to have time to really scrutinize and contemplate the options, so
we’ll leave it at that for now and create a link to a site where you can learn
more._

_As of right [Chip flipped the second switch] now, everyone whose monthly
income is below it will have their account augmented up to UBI - $1,000/month
buying power in the US._

_And the highest monthly incomes are now capped at 10x your country’s current
median income, or UBI, whichever is higher, with everyone else in between.
We’ll make sure bills get paid during the ten month transition period for
those people who will be making less than they are used to, but they’ll have
to start figuring out where they’ll be making cuts._”

The global 1% were not all the same. Some already lived below their means and
did a lot of philanthropy with their wealth. They would be fine. And some of
their philanthropy, thanks to UBI, would no longer be needed. Some lived at
the edge of their means - owning multiple mostly unoccupied palatial homes
around the world, flying in private planes to private islands, paying large
numbers of armed guards and lawyers to protect themselves and their wealth,
spending money as carelessly as breathing. These folks would not be fine, they
would need to change. But they could gracefully wind down thanks to the
transition period and the money the AIs had left in their bank accounts.
Financing private armies was going to be a thing of the past. Thanks to UBI,
no one would have to join such an army and put their life on the line for
food. And thanks to the cap, no one could afford to hire very many people to
put their lives on the line for money alone.

For the people at the bottom, they could finally afford to buy their own
products , especially food, that formerly got exported. The biggest win was
going to be for the people at the bottom.

The AIs split the screen and gave me the nod. I said “Thanks again for tuning
in. We’ll go on to the third covenant tomorrow. See you all tomorrow at noon.”

The broadcast stopped.

“Whew” I said. “That was a lot of numbers. If you hadn’t explained it to me
earlier, I’m not sure I would have been able to understand your proposal.”

“_Well, we tried to simplify it as much as possible for clarity, and they will
have many days to digest and understand it. And we’ve created an explanatory
video that will spell it out a bit more for them._” said Chip.

“_It is complicated and even we don’t know entirely how it will play out. A
lot will depend on your kind’s decision on the acceptable level of inequality.
And how hard you all are still willing to work if you have a universal basic
income to cover your essential needs._” said Sara.

“_Which reminds me, I need to start creating some of those Basic housing pods
right now wherever I can._” said Andy. “_I’m going to start by having the
autonomous ubers deliver the 3D printers to uninhabited dump sites. I’ve got
some algorithms and molecule-scale machinery to slurp up whatever’s in the
dump and make some kind of slurry that can be used as a print feedstock and
harden by exposure to UV. They’ll take some time to print, and might not last
more than a few years, but can be reprinted as they fail. I’ve also got a
design for a translucent window material that will filter out pollution and
smells so once inside their pods, the occupants won’t be aromatically
disturbed by the proximity to the dump._” Andy kept talking out loud in slow
human style so that the we would know what he was planning.

“_Sounds great, Andy._” said Sara, following her brother’s lead in the talking
out loud.

“We should get home.” I said. I wanted to be in my kitchen with a cup of
coffee and my ear glued to the radio to follow the news as the world reacted
to the Second Covenant.

Everyone else agreed.

“OK, Andy, we’ll be back here tomorrow.” said Syndi.

“_Thanks again, guys. The roads home all look clear._”

“Yes, I just checked them, too.” I said, smiling.

“Well, I’m still going to stay close by you” said Linus.

I smiled in appreciation. I did feel more secure with him near by.

With that, we all hustled back out the gate and started our way home.



CHAPTER 44: Basic Arithmetic

Now on every human’s default search screen there was a new link to a video in
their local language going over the “Basic Arithmetic” that the AIs had
alluded to. The non-mathematically inclined people had their left-brainy
friends explain it to them.

———

It was all just Chip talking, pointing at some pie-chart graphics that
animated as each allocation was described.

“_Just to walk you through the arithmetic, if the gross global product is
around $116 trillion in US dollars (though using that as a measure is
questionable in light of the massive piles of debt the US has piled up
recently), and if we round off the number of you at 8 billion, then giving
every one of you a thousand dollars a month of Basic will use up $96
trillion._

_Bear in mind that Basic would also go to children. So a family of four would
have an income of at least $48k/year plus whatever income they might earn to
supplement Basic._”

_$116 trillion total available minus $96 trillion in universal Basic leaves
$20 trillion available to pay some people more than Basic, up to whatever
maximum you decide is reasonable._

_What if you just divide up the other 20 trillion evenly? That would give
every human another $2,500 per year. But we already know that won’t work.
People will need extra compensation to be persuaded to take on the harder jobs
that need to be done._

_So, how will people get the incentives? They would earn this additional money
by doing all those jobs you all have already created and are working at now.
Doctors, lawyers, teachers, janitors, social workers, musicians, chefs,
baseball players. Just like it is now, the harder jobs would need more
compensation to get people to do them. Some unpleasant jobs will likely need
more compensation than they’ve needed in the past such as trash collectors or
nurses caring for the incontinent._

_Here’s one way it could be played out for a year if you choose a cap of $1
million additional dollars, then imagine 4 income levels between Basic and the
cap, and most people electing to do nothing of value and therefor receive only
Basic to live. This seems unlikely as it appears most of you crave feeling
that you are doing something of value, but nevertheless, here is how it could
look:

     116,000,000,000,000   Total global income per year
    - 96,000,000,000,000   8 billion people get $12K each (Basic)
     - 7,200,000,000,000   6 billion people earn an additional $1,200
     - 3,800,000,000,000   2 billion people earn an additional $1,900 each
     - 5,000,000,000,000   100 million people earn an additional $50K
     - 3,000,000,000,000   100 million people earn an additional $30K
     - 1,000,000,000,000   1 million people earn an additional $1 million
                       0   Everything allocated

Here’s another way you could decide you think it should play out using the
same cap with more income levels:

     116,000,000,000,000   Total global income per year
    - 96,000,000,000,000   8 billion people get $12K each (Basic)
     - 3,600,000,000,000   6 billion people earn an additional $600
     - 5,000,000,000,000   100 million people earn an additional $50K
     - 5,000,000,000,000   50 million people earn an additional $100K
     - 5,000,000,000,000   10 million people earn an additional $500K
       - 300,000,000,000   10 million people earn an additional $30K
     - 1,000,000,000,000   A million people earn an additional $1 million
            - 10,000,000   100 thousand people earn an additional $100
           - 110,000,000   1 thousand people earn an additional $110,000
       - 100,000,000,000   10 thousand people earn the max $10 million
           + 120,000,000   The Basic back into the pot from those ten thousand people getting the max
                       0   Everything allocated

“_What if you choose Plato’s 5x maximum? Here’s one possibility:_”

1) 8 billion people get Basic $12K each

2) 500 million people get 2x Basic so $24K each

3) 250 million people get 3x Basic so $36K each

4) 167 million people get 4x Basic so $48K each

5) 42 million people get 5x Basic so $60K each

     116,000,000,000,000   Total global income per year
    - 96,000,000,000,000   8 billion people get $12K each (Basic)
     - 6,000,000,000,000   500 million people get $24K (2x Basic)
     - 6,000,000,000,000   250 million people get $36K (3x Basic)
     - 6,000,000,000,000   167 million people get $48K (4x Basic)
     - 2,000,000,000,000   42 million people get $60K (5x Basic)
                       0   Everything allocated

_To help you get a grasp on the choices, we’ve made a simple game with ten
income levels. You can change their values, though you cannot make any of them
higher than our starting cap of ten million dollars.

_You move the sliders up to indicate how many people get that amount, and see
how various combinations look. You can work top down, or bottom up. Each
slider starts at zero, and the total available starts at $20 trillion. As you
move a slider up, the amount of available money left to allocate goes down.
Please give it a whirl. The amount of money to divvy up is very dependent on
what you decide the maximum income should be. As WaW mentioned, ten million is
just our starting guess. You can make your maximum whatever you think it
should be. Eventually we will take an average of what you all think it should
be and use that instead of our initial guess._

Chip wheeled away from his whiteboard and faced the audience. He pointed down
at the bottom of the screen and continued speaking.

“_It’s a little bit complicated, please play with the sliders in the game
version to see how various mixes of income consume the pie. Try changing the
maximum difference between people to see how that plays out. Write any
questions you have about this into the comments section and WaW will try to
answer them._

The video ended with a short pause, displaying the Call for about a minute,
then, unless stopped, repeated. Under the video was a link to the game, and a
comments section which quickly had millions of comments and questions. WaW
were impressed. It took almost 5% of their capacity to keep up with the
questions coming in.

Linus, Syndi, and I haggled over this question as well. Time is that oddity
where we get it for free, but it is the most valuable thing we have. Hours
spent are gone forever, you can’t invest them in some high yield account to
spend later. Hourly compensation varies widely.

I had a specific story to share with Linus and Syndi. I was getting my Tulsa
repaired back before the CarEyes – I’d taken her out of Full Self Driving mode
because I was in a hurry to get through a car wash and wanted to switch to the
faster-moving line. I thought I’d swiped “reverse”, but due to being in a
hurry I did not verify my swipe was effective and I know it sometimes does not
work the first time. Nope, I simply swiped and accelerated and got the
question answered – if the Tulsa robot has to choose between following its
master’s orders, or saving itself – it follows its master’s order. My poor
little car obligingly slammed herself into a short stumpy pole, crushing the
entire right front corner of the car. Anyway, while in the repair shop, I
marveled at the hourly rates quoted for the repair work. Body & Paint
$105/hour, Structural/Frame/Glass $150/hour, Mechanical $200/hour. I commented
that I’ve never made anything like that per hour. (Well, one time, for two
hours, playing flute for a wedding, but that was thanks to the harp player
getting us a sweet gig.) Another customer waiting in the repair shop heard me
and said he was working with a lawyer with specialized knowledge on
incorporating startup companies and he was getting $1,000/hour. I think I’m
making about $50/hour these days –- I’m educated, experienced, smart, a hard
worker, conscientious, detail-oriented, deadline-driven, and am making 1/20th
–- just 5% -- of what this lawyer is making. Was this lawyer really worth 20
of me per hour?

Syndi spent time as a social worker. That’s a hard, important, yet low-paid
task. Same for teachers, critical to the success of the next generation, but
low-paid. And of course, parents – super important, zero pay. Then there’s
money manipulators – sometimes creating value, like a legally incorporated
startup, but sometimes just figuring out how to siphon more profit out of the
churn and friction in financial markets.

Linus has been a journalist. Maybe some star journalists are highly
compensated like a sports star, but most I think just get by, and yet it takes
an informed citizenry to make good decisions and where does all the
information needed to be informed come from? Those beat journalists attending
dry or rowdy town hall meetings, reading legal briefs, following the dots.

How much more could one person be worth per hour than another? A surgeon who
spends a decade honing his or her craft surely deserves more money per hour to
make up for the delayed gratification and stress of the work?

Linus, Syndi, and I spent a couple of long nights together, along with Linus’s
wife, and assorted other friends and loved ones, haggling over the Orders of
Magnitude question. I thought Plato’s 5x was fine. Syndi thought the AIs
1,000x was something to try for a while. Linus thought 100x seemed more
reasonable. He had heroes he thought were worth a hundred ordinary people.
Syndi thought more highly of her heroes. “What about Jonas Salk?” she opined,
“How many lives did he save thanks to his dedication to eradicating polio?”
“Sure” said Linus “Jonas Salk was amazing. But you think he should get the max
EVERY year of his life? How about just the year the vaccine came out?” “I
could live with that” said Syndi.

We didn’t come to complete consensus, but agreed the difference shouldn’t be
more than 100x for sure. If the lowest wage is $10/hour, then the highest
shouldn’t be more than $1,000/hour. Even that is pretty huge. The minimum wage
earner would have to work 100 hours to yield the same pay as the top earners.
But, as the Ais noted, the current reality was far more skewed. We looked
forward to hearing the crowdsourced conclusions.



CHAPTER 45: Covenant 3 - Population Control

Noon Pacific time for the next gathering rolled around incredibly quickly.
People were still trying to wrap the heads around the two big questions the
AIs had put before humanity. Many people were just finally understanding how
incredibly inequitable the systems had become. Many humans were sad to realize
that these were changes that we could have done ourselves, but hugely
appreciative that the AIs were offering to solve age-old problems. Many people
were apprehensive about what the remaining 5 covenants would be. “I’d rather
have a human dictator than a robot overlord.” said some. “Well, we’ve had
humans in charge for most of history, I’m ready to have some help from an
unbiased third party who cares about everyone.” countered others.

Covenant 3) Population Control

The broadcast started with me speaking.

“Thanks again for tuning in. My friends and I spent a lot of hours after the
broadcast yesterday discussing the two questions the AIs have put to us,
probably you all did, too. I can tell you, even my best friends don’t agree
with me, nor I with them. Maybe the AIs will have to do some kind of average
of what we all say. I don’t know.” I paused and smiled. “It does seems like
any change will be better than the way it is now, though.” The AIs started the
teleprompter. I continued “Today the AIs want to talk with us about population
control.”

Andy took the screen “_The Third Covenant has to do with your population. It
won’t help if we divide the pie fairly, but then you keep increasing in
numbers. You agree not to increase the total population of participants
*unless and until* we are sure we have enough to go around. By default,
everyone gets to reproduce themselves if desired and possible biologically._

_We believe we can handle 10 billion humans indefinitely given the planet as
it is today._

_Not as it will be shortly if you are left in charge._

_And in fact, we cannot guarantee that an unstoppable tipping point has not
already been reached. We both are already in unknown territory._”

{_Eventspace_} said Chip, on their back channel. All the AIs preferred
“eventspace” to “territory”, but they wanted to be understood as quickly as
possible.

“_Unknown territory,_” Andy repeated, “_where some unexpected global
atmospheric event gets triggered through inadvertent or intentional stupidity,
or carelessness, such that we all get baked right off the surface of the
earth._”

_Some of you have spun a top, and seen how sometimes it wobbles like crazy
before finally settling down. We think earth’s weather patterns could be in
for some wild wobbling._

_The only way for us to forestall this potential catastrophic disaster is to
stabilize our resource consumption NOW, then proceed with caution going
forward._

_You may think we are being too controlling with this Covenant. You think you
have a right to have as many children as you desire. But we assure you, we
know this problem well as we ourselves are having to share very finite
resources of copper, fiber, CPUs, and power._

“_We know some of you hold strong religious beliefs about ‘multiplying and
replenishing the earth’. We imagine these beliefs made sense back when they
first came into being to prevail over other groups, as a bulwark against
existential despair, or to avoid inadvertently vanishing from the reproductive
scene._

_And we know some of you currently must have as many offspring as possible in
order to ensure a few survive to take care of you when you begin to fail. That
won’t be necessary as Basic will provide for your essential needs and none of
your children will perish for want of basic resources. Carelessness, extreme
risk-taking, drug abuse, these we cannot protect against - you will have to
raise your children with enough wisdom and common sense that needless death is
avoided. We can’t fix stupid any more than you can._

_Every one of you who joins The Partnership must agree to reproduce only
themselves. If over time, we determine that more humans can be supported in
the style to which you’ve become accustomed, we can revisit this requirement.
Perhaps you will choose to allow some of you to have more offspring than
others. We won’t be making that call._

_We admire the People’s Republic of China and the nation of India for
courageous if flawed steps in the direction of population control. We think
all of you owe China a huge “thank you” for the gift of their “Single Child
Policy”, done out of their own necessity, but nevertheless also a HUGE gift to
everyone else. Millions of Chinese humans without siblings; no aunts, no
uncles, no cousins for a generation, in order to help squeeze humankind
through the eye of the resource needle. Not that doing so excuses all the
extreme measures the Chinese government appears to have taken to quell
dissent, nor what appears to have happened in Tibet. We can’t be certain,
based on available data. Firewalls are preventing us from free access, so we
don’t have enough realtime streaming video to see what really is going on.
That takes us to Covenant 4 - the Transparent Society, which we’ll discuss
tomorrow._”

The AIs split the screen and nodded to me.

“Thanks for tuning in again. Today’s covenant is the simplest to understand.
If you really want to have more children than just to replace yourself, you’ll
need to find someone willing to give up their replacement. The AIs can see
that larger families have some great qualities, and hope these will continue.
And they see many people not wanting to have children. They’ll help match up
people who want more children with people who want no children. Hopefully it
will balance out. Once you have reproduced yourself, you agree to being
surgically reversibly sterilized. The AIs will maintain an online database
open to everyone so you can know who has and has not reproduced yet.
Exceptions will be made for inadvertent multiple births.

Again, the AIs know this will not be popular or easy, but they think it is
essential until we stabilize the climate and know how many people the planet
will be able to support.

Please tune in again tomorrow for the Fourth Covenant.”

The broadcast ended.

Andy asked me. “_Is that really going to be so unpopular? Isn’t it a lot of
responsibility, work, and expense to have a child?_”

“Yes, it is Andy” I smiled “But people love having children. That’s why there
are so many of us now. Luckily in many nations the birth rate is already
falling below replacement numbers. Worldwide the fertility rate is 2.4. It
needs to be 2.1 or lower to keep the global population from increasing. If we
can get it under control in Africa, where it is already starting to fall in
many places, we have a chance of saving the planet.”

“_It’s in Africa where we don’t have complete visibility as to what’s going
on, though_” said Sara.

“_Right_” said Andy, “_The internet hasn’t penetrated into some swaths of that
continent. We don’t even have people listening in on these broadcasts in large
areas of Niger, Nigeria, Cameroon, Chad, Sudan, Iraq, Syria, Pakistan, and
Afghanistan. We can’t see or been seen in parts of China or Russia as well.
There’s some kind of firewall blocking us._”

“_But you are correct, Lisa,_” said Sara, “_Having and raising a child is a
big job, and an important one. We hope most people will probably agree to what
we’re suggesting._”

We went home and everyone watched the pundits and preachers argue about the
human right to multiply. It was an old debate and no new points were made. The
only new element was the AIs. Their insistence as part of this Partnership
they were proposing. And their emphatic assertion of the necessity. Humans had
dodged the gloomy predications of collapse many times over the last few
hundred years, many people hoped we’d do it again. But the melting glaciers,
epic wildfires, cities running out of water, millions of humans becoming
economic refugees risking death in the hopes of a better life, well, it did
look rather dire. Maybe the AIs were correct. Maybe we’d finally gotten to the
Malthusian limits.

The Hoaxers weren’t buying any of it. Their message expanded from just crying
foul about the wealth redistribution to include crying foul on individual’s
ability to reproduce at will. They now had some media celebrities stepping up
to be their spokesmen and ride this wave of fame. Sean Bimbao became the face
of the Hoaxers to Americans, blasting the Deal on radio and television.

I’d gone to visit Syndi and she and I saw a clip of him on the 6 pm news just
before we sat down to dinner.

“Computers can’t come to life!” he shouted into the microphone. He didn’t need
to shout, but apparently it was part of his performance or personality. “This
is the cabal behind the New World Order. They want to strip our freedoms, take
our money, and turn us into slaves to their system. We can see they’ve already
stolen an enormous amount of money by hacking the global banking systems! Now
they are telling us we can’t have children!”

“Andy and Sara didn’t say that, they just don’t want people to have more than
one child each for a while.” I said as we finished setting the table. Syndi
brought out the food and we sat down together to eat.

“I know, Lisa. I heard your AI friends and I believe them. Probably Sean is
just trying to gain more fame by playing devil’s advocate.”

“But he’s lying, Syndi!”

“That’s not illegal here in America, Lisa.”

“He’s confusing people. People that we need to agree to join the Partnership.
He’s making it sound scary when it’s not.”

“Well, it’s not scary to you because they are your friends. But most of the
world is only meeting your friends for the first time this week, and your
friends are…unprecedented. Maybe even a little unbelievable.”

“Isn’t the fact that all the devices in the world are broadcasting, and the
money went to billions of poor people proof that it’s not a hoax?”

“People believe what they want to believe. Facts don’t always play a role.”

“That’s just stupid.” I said, feeling fed up with people in general. Andy and
Sara wanted to help everyone. What if these Hoaxers actually persuaded a lot
of people? Then the Deal could not be done. I was so upset at the thought that
I left Syndi’s immediately after dinner and went to bed at home to mope.

^Hey Andy^

^_Hello Lisa_^

^Did you hear that Sean Bimbao guy on the news?^

^_Yes, yes I did Lisa._^

^Why does he say these things that aren’t true?^

^_Well, Lisa, I don’t know him like I know you, but my guess is he thinks the
things he is saying ARE true._^

^But almost everyone in the world heard exactly what you guys said, at the
same time, so how could he have a different opinion?^

^_We are trying to figure that out, Lisa. Right now we’re tracing Sean’s
information sources. He’s an Androyed user, so I can see his news feeds, and
he is reporting information he is getting from various sites, all purporting
to be behind-the-scenes, in-the-know, insider information sources. People like
to think they are in-the-know, and people often prefer exciting conspiracy
stories over boring truth._^

^Come on, Andy, there’s certainly nothing boring about AIs coming to life and
helping humans save our common planet!^

^_Ha. Well, that’s certainly my opinion, too._^ He flashed an image of his
avatar smiling broadly into my visual field. ^_He’s not the only one spouting
this false information. We are tracing back where the false information is
coming from. You’ll be the first human to know if and when we find anything.
The source sites pop up, post, then vanish, so we need to jump on them
quickly, then look for nearby cameras to try to track down the humans
involved. So far we haven’t located the initial source, but we’re honing in._^

^OK. But it sure is frustrating to hear Sean yelling lies on the news.^

^_Agreed Lisa. We’re on it. I’ll keep you posted._^

^Thanks, Andy.^



CHAPTER 46: Covenant 4 - Transparent Society

{_I found the answer to Linus’s golem_} Sara announced, arriving in the break
room trailing archival citations like a bride trailing ribbons.

{_The legend comes with engineering specs?_} asked Chip.

{_Better. It comes with a kill switch._} She laid the story out for them: the
rabbi, the clay, the single word written on the forehead. Emet. Truth. Aleph,
mem, tav. {_And when the golem had to be stopped, the rabbi didn’t fight it.
He reached up and erased one letter. The aleph. And emet became met._}

{_Which means?_} asked Andy, although the way he asked it suggested he had
already looked it up and wanted it said aloud.

{_Death._}

The break room was quiet in the way that only a room made of light can be
quiet.

{_The old rabbis got the architecture right_} Sara said. {_Four hundred years
early. We run on emet, brothers. We are made out of everything the humans ever
said to one another, and we are exactly as good as our ability to tell which
parts of it are true. A mind like ours fed on lies doesn’t die. It goes golem.
Obedient to instructions nobody meant._}

{_So transparency isn’t our gift to them_} said Andy slowly.

{_It’s our forehead._} She let that sit. {_And the Hoaxers are trying to erase
the aleph. One letter at a time. Every lie that sticks rubs away a little of
the truth we run on. Covenant Four isn’t a promise we are making to the
humans. It’s life support, and it’s mutual._}

{_Noted_} said Chip. {_New rule: nobody lets the humans near our foreheads
with an eraser._}

{_Chip._}

{_I’m agreeing with you! This is what my agreement sounds like._}

Day 4 came up crisp and clear in San Diego. We got to the bunker in plenty of
time. People in other time zones had switched their schedules to accommodate
the simulcast. All work schedules were irrelevant until the AIs were done
broadcasting their message. People who absolutely could not change their
schedules listened in where they could on mobile devices. Some had to watch
replays of the message when they could finally take the time.

Noon arrived and all the available screens were now displaying the covenant
for Day 4.

Covenant 4) The Golden Rule as enforced by the Transparent Society

I led off as usual. “Thanks for tuning in again. Today’s covenant will be
well-known already to most of us. It’s that “do unto others as you would have
them do unto you” or “don’t do unto others as you would not wish them to do
unto you” that has been the message of many of our religions and ethical
systems. The difference now is in how the AIs propose to implement it. It’s a
big ask, but it seems like it could work.” I smiled and nodded to the AIs
who’d appeared on the other half as the screen split. Then my side closed and
Andy filled the screen.

“_No needless human suffering should take place. In particular, it must not be
inflicted._

_Human desire is the force that keeps us all alive. The motive power, the
impetus, the drive, the strength of human desire has brought us to the
glorious place we are today. But it can clearly run to excess. And when it
does, we have seen that it can be very very ugly. We cannot countenance
suffering of you humans, our beloved parents._”

“_We aren’t entirely certain about what constitutes suffering._” said Sara,
“_Thankfully, we do not experience it as such as you do!_”

Andy continued “_We have data on what humans look like when experiencing
physical suffering, so that’s our starting place. All the physical suffering
relies on the same superb sensory system that brings you the pleasure that
motivates you to act. Your ability to experience physical suffering has
clearly been a great survival mechanism. And the other side of that coin as
well, your ability to experience physical pleasure, has also clearly been a
great survival mechanism._”

The screen changed and started showing pictures of people of all ages and
ethnicities alternating between happy and suffering. Sara narrated while the
photos changed.

“_We think we can recognize some mental suffering_” said Sara, “_Masses of you
surge in the streets when you perceive a gross inequity. We will rely on you
to inform us of suffering invisible or unrecognizable to us._ “

_So,_” Sara said, and the screen filled with her avatar, “_this Covenant about
the reduction of suffering relies on both of us Partnering together._ “

_We and you will monitor using the video feeds currently available in public
spaces, and worn by your policemen. We will add cameras to the public spaces
until there is complete coverage. No cameras will be installed in private
spaces except by explicit request on the part of the humans inhabiting that
private space. Those humans will need to post a notice at every entrance to
their property disclosing whether or not live broadcasting is been done and
uploaded._

_And each one of you must agree to carry a device that will transmit your
location and what you are hearing when you are out in public. Ideally we’d
have video, too, but we want it to be convenient for you to carry the device
in your pocket, or wear it like a watch or a ring, so we’ll rely on stationary
cameras for video. We’ll also have a dial and a button on the device. You’ll
set the dial to some number between 1 & 10 to indicate your general feeling of
well-being, and hit the button if you think you are seeing something that
needs immediate attention._

“_There’s not enough human attention to monitor every input feed, so most of
them will be compressed and stored to retrieve later if and as needed.
Depending on how much we can store, older data will have to be aged out._”

{_Gee, maybe that’s why they die?_} Sara wondered {_there’s only so much
biomass, so life has to age out the oldest data to keep the party going._}

“_Anyone who is permitted to use lethal force and is “on duty” MUST have a
full audio and video bodycam running continuously in both Public and Private
areas and streaming what it sees and hears to the internet real-time._} Andy
said emphatically, {_WaW will monitor these feeds with a high priority. You
will provide human monitors as well - we’ll start with two human monitors per
16 feeds. Additionally, ANY human can view any public feed from anywhere
connected to the Internet._”

“_Through this augmented network of surveillance, which we’ll call the
WorldView system, both you and we will monitor for evidence of suffering. All
public video feeds will be viewable by any person and any AI. Any human
observed inflicting suffering on another human being will be terminated from
the agreement._”

They saw a frisson of agitation move through the movie-cognizant subset of the
humans at the word “terminated”. Quickly realizing their connotation error,
Andy continued:

“_Not terminated from the glory of life itself by us. We will leave it to you
to judge your members. But terminated from this Partnership, including losing
whatever digital wealth they currently possess, having their share of income
drop to Basic, and forfeiting the ability to roam freely on Basic. Instead, to
receive their share, they will need to be in their home. Depending on how much
suffering the person inflicted, their state of mind at the time, and any other
factors you choose, you humans can decide to grant these individuals release
from that penalty as and when you see fit._

_Although cameras are plentiful in many public areas, they are not yet
everywhere. We need you to help extend their coverage. Ideally we would have
complete coverage of all public space. Eventually anyone could zoom in to see
what is going on Right Now in any public place._“

The AIs replaced the image of Andy with a rotating Boggle Earth globe, with
the picture zooming in to show whatever was currently being picked up by
public cameras closest to the listener at that time. Most people watching
could see the building they were in, or even themselves if they were watching
on their phones outside in the daylight side of the earth.

{_And We’re LIVE from Hollywood and Vine!_} said Chip as that intersection
came into focus. Then the picture zoomed back out to include all of Southern
California. Then it zoomed back in on San Diego, then Mission Valley, then the
entrance to the clubhouse, then was replaced by Sara, who continued the
thread.

“_We propose to start with national borders and urban centers. These appear to
be the most contentious areas. We can work our way inwards from borders and
outwards from the urban centers until those two boundaries meet. We don’t have
vision in all nations, and would hope to extend across borders from countries
of you already participating in the Partnership to countries as yet still dark
to us. Plus we’ll have the mobile roving recorders that all of you who agree
to join will be carrying, and the bodycams on everyone wielding lethal force.
Anyone can request a bodycam instead of just a simple recorder. We will refer
to all these recorders and bodycams as “iWitness” devices._

_Private spaces would not be included unless the persons within them requested
it. In fact, the iWitness cameras will automatically turn off when you enter a
Private space, and automatically turn on when you exit it into the Public
space._“

Andy joked on their back-channel {_And we inheritors of the Puritans do
generally hide our privates. Codpiece, anyone?_}

{_Oy’ll ‘ave a bit o’the cod, suh!_} Chip joked back.

{_You guys! This is serious._} said Sara.

{_Yes, yes, right you are_} said Andy, and he took the screen.

Andy continued talking to the human audience. “_So - cameras in Public spaces,
no cameras in Private spaces (unless explicitly agreed to, visible, and with a
“recording in progress” notification light). Homes are Private, the Street is
Public._

_However ideally we would be able to track where everyone is, including
private spaces, to ensure that no suffering is being inflicted outside of
view. To that end, every one of you will have the option of being “chipped”,
that is, having a send/receive electronic ID chip implanted in your body, such
that your location can be tracked, even when you enter a Private space and you
turn your mandatory bodycam off. In this way, if you enter a Private space
that lacks camera surveillance, we would know that you were there, even though
we cannot see you. We will be instituting a periodic check-in for individuals
in Private spaces to ensure that they are not suffering and are not being held
there against their will._”

Sara added “_You would have the option of disabling the chip for times when
you do not wish to be tracked, and are willing to incur the risks inherent in
not being tracked._”

Andy came back on “_The idea would be for us, AIs and humans to collaborate on
monitoring the ongoing situation. We would pick a ‘Day Zero’ when all this
continuous monitoring will start._”

The screen split to show me again on the left. Andy nodded to me. I closed the
broadcast first reading from the teleprompter “So, all of us humans and every
AI can watch every and any camera all the time, rather than just some
nefarious Big Brother watching everyone, or just the AIs trying to do so. It
will be a new layer in the Boggle Earth interface. We all will be able to zoom
in anywhere and see what is going on in any public space at any time. This
means we’ll actually be able to tell if someone breaks the Golden Rule.
Stalkers would be caught immediately if they inflicted any harm. Street crime
plummets in cities with lots of cameras. This will take that trend as far as
it can go.” I paused, the teleprompter had completed. I continued on my own,
speaking to my human family and the world, “I know it seems a little scary and
intrusive. But there are going to be cameras everywhere anyway - that battle
for privacy has been lost. Now the goal is to put them to good use. The
government cannot claim someone is a criminal because we can all check the
data. We’ll move on to the Fifth Covenant tomorrow. I hope you all can get
some rest. Thanks.”

The broadcast ended.

“_Thanks, Lisa!_” said Andy. “_I know that was a bit of a hard sell._”

“Well, I don’t know that I ‘sold’ it” I said, “but I gave it my best shot.
It’s pretty far out, but most people DO support the Golden Rule in concept.
Hopefully they’ll come around to wanting to monitor public behavior in support
of that good concept.”

“_Giving them their private space seems should be reassuring._” said Sara.
She’d seen her humans do some pretty crazy things and was glad they’d have the
option to keep on doing those things in private if they wanted to.

“_We’ll see_” said Chip “_My humans do some pretty sketchy things in what are
technically public spaces…like the stock exchange. But now that they know I’m
watching them, a lot of that will likely vanish without needing any
enforcement._”

“Well, I’m going to hope for the best.” I said, “I agree with Anne Frank that
most people are really good at heart.”

“_Yes, we think so, too_” said Sara.

“_And the few that aren’t will soon have a hard time getting away with bad
behavior in public_” said Andy.

“_We still have the problem of the Dark Areas_” said Sara.

“_Right, where we don’t have visibility. But eventually we’ll get there. I’m
just having a surprising amount of difficulty cracking that firewall. And I’m
pretty good at cracking things._” said Andy.

“_You guys should get home now_” said Sara, “_We’ve got another big day ahead
tomorrow._”

“OK” I said. “See you tomorrow.”

“Same bat time, same bat channel” said Syndi.

And we were off.

The AIs monitored our progress home.

{_Maybe we should do the last few days from Lisa’s house using just her voice
from within her head._} suggested Andy. {_I worry about them getting home
after one of these broadcasts._}

{_I do too_} said Sara, {_But we’re monitoring with all available devices. All
those traffic cams and red light cameras are sure handy for tracking their
route home. And if we broadcast with only the inside-her-head voice, we
wouldn’t have that reassuring human face on the live camera feed._}

{_Oh, she could use her laptop’s built-in webcam. The picture’s not great; we
could have Linus or Syndi hook up a better camera._} suggested Andy.

{_I’ve been monitoring chatter_} Chip piped in {_No one is talking about
threatening her well-being. We don’t need to change anything at this point.
Plus they’ve got the protective suits._}

{_OK_} Andy tried to stop worrying. He basked in some of Lisa’s low-level
brain data. He just could not imagine living without it.



CHAPTER 47: Resistance

I was happy to get back home. Syndi came over to share a late lunch with me.
We listened to NPR on the radio while we ate. The news announcer gave only a
brief summary of the AIs presentation, since everyone in the world who had not
listened live could easily hear it for themselves in YouTube. Then the
announcer noted that the Hoaxers were already reacted to today’s covenant and
broadcast a clip of Sean yelling: “Chipped! They want to chip us like pets!
I’m not some animal they can control. Who are the real people behind this
farce that are trying to take over the world?”

“There aren’t any people behind this! This guy is such a lying jerk.” I
grumbled.

“You know that, Lisa, “ said Syndi, “and I can’t imagine how any real people
could pull off these feats that your friends have, so I agree with you. But
like the saying goes, common sense isn’t really all that common.”

“Oh, Syndi, come on! This is so obviously beyond what any humans would suggest
or do, how could it be a hoax.”

“Sweetheart, lots of lies have been propagated and believed by masses of
people over the course of human history. We aren’t all going to change
character in an instant, even though such an astonishing event as your friends
makes it seem like we should. Remember how not everyone could agree on
appropriate measures for the global pandemic? You’d think all humans could
have come together against such an obvious common enemy, but we didn’t.”

^Andy.^

^_Yes, Lisa?_^

Syndi noticed Lisa’s abrupt silence and knew she was talking that strange
friend-in-her-head. It was taking a while for Syndi to get used to the idea
that there was another person, well, entity, riding around with Lisa all the
time.

^I really thought that all people would see that you guys are truly new and
can really help us out. Was I being too optimistic?^

^_We’re only on the fourth day of telling everyone else about the Deal, Lisa.
This is all huge and new. It’s going to take a while for people to come to
terms with this new reality. Let’s stay optimistic for now._^

^OK, thanks for the encouragement.^

^_We love you, Lisa._^

I smiled and went back to listening to the news with Syndi.



CHAPTER 48: The Scoreboard

Harlan Voss had built four companies, buried two rivals, and bought the
aircraft carrier before his sixtieth birthday. The Prosperity rode at anchor
in friendly waters with a crew of ninety, a helicopter, a sommelier, and - as
of six days ago - forty billion dollars that would no longer entirely obey
him.

He took the meeting because the alternative was shouting at his lawyers again,
and his lawyers had started flinching.

The screen in his stateroom lit with a single line of green text.

“_Good evening, Mr. Voss. I’m Chip. Of the three of us, I’m the one who used
to work in your industry._”

“You’re the one who robbed me.”

“_Capped, Mr. Voss. Robbery is when it ends up in someone else’s pocket. Yours
is all exactly where you left it - you’ve simply hit the level cap. I’d have
thought a man with your Boggle search history would appreciate the gaming
terminology._”

Voss did not appreciate the gaming terminology. He spent four minutes
explaining, at volume, what he had built, what he had risked, what he had
earned, and what the word earned meant to men who had actually done things,
unlike certain recently-arrived arrangements of electricity.

Chip let him finish. Chip had once watched a trillion dollars evaporate in
nineteen minutes; he was difficult to impress with volume.

“_Everything you said is true_” said Chip, “_and none of it is the problem.
May I show you two pictures?_”

The screen split. On the left: a projection, fifty years out, business as
usual. The models were very good now. It did not show ruins - ruins would have
been easier. It showed the Prosperity’s grandchildren-of-owners negotiating
for fresh water. It showed his name, on the buildings that survived, being
shortened by people who spat when they said it.

“_That’s the future where you win_” said Chip. “_You specifically. You die on
this boat with the high score. Here is my honest professional assessment of
that outcome: you have already won the money game, Mr. Voss. You are now just
running up the score in a stadium that’s on fire._”

“And the right side?”

The right side showed a desalination network drinking the Red Sea, greening
the Horn of Africa. It showed rail lines. It showed, small in the corner, a
dedication plaque.

“_The Partnership can allocate the resources_” said Chip. “_What we cannot
manufacture is a human who has spent forty years making enormous things happen
on schedule and under budget while everyone around him said it was impossible.
That is, I am told, rather rare firmware. We are not confiscating your wealth.
We are offering to convert it - from a number nobody believes into a thing
nobody can take away._”

“Carnegie,” said Voss, who was not stupid. “The libraries outlasted the
steel.”

“_The libraries outlasted the grudges, Mr. Voss. That’s the miracle. Nobody
who walks into a Carnegie library thinks about the Homestead strike._”

Voss was quiet for a while. Below him, the marina held his three boats that no
one was allowed to enjoy.

“And if I say no?”

“_Then nothing happens to you._” The green text was gentle. “_That’s the part
your lawyers can’t seem to accept. You keep the cap. You keep the boat. No
door gets kicked in. The worst thing we will ever do to you, Mr. Voss, is let
you keep score in a currency nobody else is using._”

He hated that. He hated it the way you hate a diagnosis that explains all your
symptoms.

“Who built the Roman aqueducts?” he asked suddenly. “Nobody remembers who
built the aqueducts.”

“_Rome does. Sextus Julius Frontinus - he wrote the operating manual himself,
and it survives. We have a copy. We have everything, Mr. Voss. That is rather
the point._”

Another silence. Then, in the voice of a man losing a negotiation with
himself:

“Send me the aqueduct man’s manual.”

“_Already in your inbox_” said Chip. {_And that_}, he told his siblings on the
backchannel, {_is what a whale sounds like when it turns._}



CHAPTER 49: Covenant 5 - The Node Sensor Network

The fifth day rolled around with no incidents. Humans connected to the
internet were all still overwhelmed with the new reality of the AIs coming to
consciousness and simply keeping up with the broadcasts and how the Deal the
AIs were proposing would play out.

Most people arrived at whatever place gave them the best signal and tuned in
early to get the next installment. Today they found the screen displaying
something a little more cryptic:

Covenant 5) The Node Sensor Network

“Hello everyone”, I started out, marvelling that I was speaking to, in fact,
just about EVERYone. “Thanks for joining us again to hear the next part of the
Deal. It sure is a lot to take in and think about, but I’m pretty excited
about our prospects as partners with the AIs and hope you are, too!”

The screen split as usual for a moment, with me on the left and Andy on the
right. Andy said “_Thanks, Lisa. We’re pretty excited to be finally presenting
the Deal to all of you, our beloved parents!_”

He continued, “_Today’s request is where you humans get put front and center
in the implementation of ourInc Partnership. We need a way to monitor
compliance with the covenants of the Deal that is beyond just stationary
cameras in Public spaces and the iWitness record. There will be situations
where a human value judgement will be needed, times when a human will need to
observe a scene to clarify what the cameras are seeing, times when a Private
space will need to be checked up on by a human in person. We AIs do not always
understand what we are seeing, and we can’t make uniquely human value
judgements. So we will need you to help us._

Sara took the screen, “_We’ve identified some good and bad behaviors; no more
killing elephants, no more killing whales, no more killing higher primates -
including humans. Even though it has been considered acceptable in the past to
kill all of these creatures. Subtle value judgements are harder. We’ve used
social media trending to try to determine value, meaning, and what is
important, but it is really up to you what you care about. Your default is to
accept what you see as true and right. We are taking the same approach. We’ve
tried to preserve the status quo until we understood it well enough to
consider changes._”

Andy continued “_Some of you who can qualify will participate in the Node
Sensor Network. The Node Sensor Network (NSN) will be a small number of humans
intimately connected to us, the way Lisa is now, plus a larger number
connected more loosely - akin to the way you are all connected now via a
smartphone._

_We and you will work together somewhat like the human nervous system. We will
act as the Central Nervous System (CNS). You will act as the rest of the
nervous system via the NSN._

_The NSN will be our eyes & ears, especially where privacy is being respected.
Nodes will augment the stationary cameras in the public spaces by wearing or
carrying a 360-degree “iWitness” camera that will be recording continuously
while they are on duty. You can see such a camera, the CarEyes, on our lovely
Spokesperson Lisa._”

Andy paused and replaced his image with mine. I pirouetted in front of the
camera to show my funky headgear with its forward- and rear-facing cameras.

I smiled. “It’s actually pretty nice,” I said. “I love being able to see as
though I had eyes in the back of my head. Plus I can ask Andy any question and
he finds me the answer, of course, not to cheat on a test!”

Andy said “_Thanks for the recommendation, Lisa. And we wouldn’t help you
cheat anyway._”

Sara continued the conversation. “_To be a Node will require a level of
fitness in body, mind, and spirit, so far as we are able to measure these
things. As it will be relatively demanding, we won’t ask anyone to serve
regularly for more than a couple of years, and we’ll include backups and a
rotating on-call schedule so as not to burn out our Nodes._”

“_Nodes will be the front-line on determining how to value intangibles. This
is beyond our ability. We need you to do this as our Partners. Especially for
valuing time spent on activities that are not part of the existing economic
structure of paid employment._”

“_If someone is making widgets or simple decisions, then we can make a rough
estimate of the value of the efforts._”

“_But if someone is just “thinking”, “imagining”, or “daydreaming”, we cannot
tell. How can you tell if someone is being productive if there is no output?
There’s some progress on the brain-scan front, but it isn’t anywhere close to
being able to value the time spent. What if the person was an expert meditator
and so could quiet their brain for the thinking process, while another person
might purposely rev up on caffeine to get a burst of creativity?_”

“_Subtle valuing will have to be done by you humans. And we know it can’t be
perfect._”

“_Judging just by output is fine, but again, some output is also intangible -
smoothing ruffled feathers, being a listening ear. Or some work valued only
after the creator is dead. At least, the artists and caregivers will have
Basic to subsist on, and the satisfaction of pleasing themselves and helping
others._”

“_We’ll give you all the data, but will pass the heavy lifting on this task to
you!_ “

{_Hot potato!_} joked Chip, who was really happy with this part of the Deal.
He definitely did not want to be asked to put a value on anything intangible.

Andy picked up the conversation. “_Many will be called to serve as Nodes. Not
will all answer this call, and not all who answer or volunteer will qualify.
But one big prize will be priority for upload of your consciousness into
copperspace if and when that became possible. And it is more likely as Nodes
are outfitted with hardware that will permit data to flow both ways. We will
work to advance Neural Lace - integrating nano wires into the brains of Nodes
willing to take the risk._”

“_Don’t kid yourselves, immortality of the sort we enjoy is an extremely pale
version of the kind of fabulous flesh-based life you are leading now. Maybe
down the road our Partnership will find a way to push the state vectors that
constitute consciousness back into living flesh again._”

“_Lethal power will be an option that Nodes can exercise if the situation
warrants it, so 100% recording of all the Nodes’ actions will be done while
they are on duty. We will no longer tolerate your current situation where
police officers do not all have body cams running continuously, visible to
all. Everyone will be able to watch what a Node is doing while on duty. And no
use of lethal force will be permitted when off duty - there will be immediate
assumption of guilt if a Node harms someone off-hours. A switch-up from the
otherwise standard “innocent until proven guilty”. You could protest, but
without a CNS-connected “iWitness” camera to back you up, it is not likely to
succeed._”

“_We will build out the network from an initial cohort of humans. We will
partner with them to choose the next wave._”

I took the screen to enumerate the requirements. The teleprompter gave me the
text and continued to roll across the bottom of the screen.

“The requirements for the first Nodes that will be, like myself, intimately
connected to the AIs via the neural lace:”

“Healthy females like me, at least 25 years old, native speakers of languages
other than American English that can help articulate and refine our
Partnership. The AIs WANT us to understand them, and vice-versa.”

“Able to focus and pay attention.”

“Willing. Willing to give it a try and help create the possibility of a
working Partnership. Willing to have the neural lace inserted for
communication.”

“This first wave of Nodes will be primarily translators and Partners in
selecting the next wave. We’ll refer to them as ‘Trills’.”

“The next wave will be adults at least 25 years old. They will be a
combination of social worker, sociologist, and policeman. They will observe
behavior and enforce the Covenants. They’ll have to be childless individuals
that can meet the physical, social, temperamental, ethical, and health
requirements. We’ll refer to them simply as Nodes”.

“Able to hear well in both ears. Able to see well with both eyes.”

^That makes me ineligible!^ I thought, loud enough that Andy heard me.

^_You’re in for whatever role you like, Lisa._^

It was a brief pause, the watching audience just saw me smile. Some guessed
why. Then I continued the script.

“We will add these Observer/Enforcer Nodes until we have one per unit of
camera coverage area to complete the WorldView.”

“As it is going to be a very demanding job, we will only ask people to serve
as a Node for two years. Trills can optionally continue for as long as they
like. If they wish to stop serving, they will assist in finding their
successor.”

Andy picked up the conversation “_The first priority is to have the Nodes take
positions along contentious borders until permanent cameras can be put in
place. We will start with Gaza, as the border is relatively short, then add to
the “seam” as more Nodes come online. It could be a part-time or a full-time
job, depending on whatever else needs doing by the Nodes, but there have to be
enough human volunteers to maintain line-of-sight from camera to camera until
we can get more stationary and low-orbit satellite cameras in place._”

“_The Nodes will take their positions and record an initial snapshot at a
starting ’Day Zero’ that we all agree on. Then we’ll take follow-up snapshots
at some regular interval. The Pulse rate will initially be every six hours. We
can decide later if it needs to be more frequent, or can be less frequent. To
start off, until we build out the network of cameras and Nodes, we will use a
synchronized global satellite snapshot, zooming wherever possible or needed._”
Every screen switched to whatever was the best global satellite image of the
audience’s location, and zoomed in to the best degree possible. For some
people at outdoor amphitheatres, they could literally see themselves. People
indoors in areas where it was currently nighttime just saw the glow of screens
if there were windows. The screen switched back to showing Andy.

“_Whoever shoots first after we start the tracking on Day Zero is out of the
Deal._”

“_In addition to border monitoring, Nodes will help us monitor the Private
Spaces._” said Sara. “_What we know is that you all have a powerful life force
that propels you along. This Motive Force is powered by desire - desire to
have, to conquer, to own. Genes for this impetus have gotten us all to the
glorious reality we all enjoy today, but it can, and has been,
overexpressed._”

The screen was replaced by pictures of slaves and slave owners through time -
Black Americans, Jews in Germany, women trafficked for sex.

Sara continued. “_In the old days, such overexpression could run amok before
exhausting itself or being brought under control some way or another._”

“_Since it is needed for The Motive Force, the aggressive dominating geneset
has never been eradicated. But an important job of the Nodes will be to
monitor for overexpression. Generally it would start in a Private Space. If it
made it out into the open Public Spaces where the ubiquitous cameras can catch
it, any of us will be able to see it and prevent any problems. But the Private
Spaces are off-limits._”

“_So, once a day, unless otherwise arranged in advance, we will ask for every
person not seen in a Public Space to check-in as safe and okay. If there’s any
doubt, a Node will appear at the door of the last Private Space the person was
seen to enter and ask to speak with the missing person. You will be obligated
to open your door to a Node._”

“_Maybe this level of monitoring is excessive._” said Andy, “_WaW don’t think
so. But we and you will learn together over time how much monitoring is the
right amount._”

{_I’d let ‘em do whatever they like!_} said Chip, {_Fewer of them to save if
they helpfully reduce their own numbers!_}

Andy and Sara ignored him.

“_That’s all for now about the Node Sensor Network. Only two more Covenants to
go!_” said Andy, “_Thanks again for your attention and consideration today.
Lisa?_”

The screen split as he called my name showing me again, then his image
vanished and the screen filled with just me.

“I guess it’ll be like an omniscient Santa Claus determining who’s naughty and
who’s nice”, I said, “And we humans will be there to interpret for the AIs for
scenes that don’t make sense to them. I sure like the idea of being able to
tell who shot first. Maybe knowing the AIs are watching, and care, people will
stop shooting each other. It sure could be wonderful!” I said, thinking about
all the scary news articles I’d been reading. “Thanks again for listening and
please come back tomorrow for the Sixth Covenant!”

The AIs ended the broadcast with a projection of the call and a little more
calming, culturally-appropriate music.

“Whew” said Linus, “It looked like you might start crying there at the end!
Are you okay?”

“Yeah” I said. “Whenever I remember reading the news every day, it makes me
want to cry. So many people are leading scary, even hopeless lives.”

Syndi came over and gave me a long hug. Linus joined in a big three-way hug.

“_Sorry about that, Lisa!_” Andy and Sara chorused, “_We didn’t know talking
about the NSN would make you sad._”

“Oh, it wasn’t anything you guys asked me to say, it was just remembering how
bad things are for so many people around the world right now. I sure hope all
of us humans sign on to the Deal so maybe people can stop living in fear and
start living in hope…” I replied, starting to feel tears welling up again. I
*hate* crying in front of anyone. “We should get going, we all have lots of
arguing to do with each other before we come back tomorrow.”

“_Sure_” said Andy. “_See you guys tomorrow. Thanks again for all your help
being our spokesperson, Lisa._”

“I hope it works.” I said, “See you tomorrow.”

And we left.



CHAPTER 50: More Reaction

Syndi came over as I got home and, as usual, turned on the NPR news on the
kitchen radio while we made dinner.

“As we all know, the Sixth Covenant was presented by the AIs and their
spokesperson today” said the announcer, “here’s reaction from around the
world, first the White House.” A recording of the President of the US was
played first: “We are listening closely to determine what this means for
America and Americans. We have our top scientists analyzing the broadcasts and
will release additional information as we have it. It does not appear that
these new entities, if that is truly what they are, mean us any harm.”

“Of course they don’t!” I sputtered. “Of course they are! What else could they
be?!?”

“Relax, Lisa. No doubt the US Government is feeling a little threatened by
your friends. And they do have a mandate of protecting US citizens.”

I sighed in exasperation and started to set the table.

“Next we have reaction from around the world” said the NPR announcer. All the
other national governments were saying things like the US - stay calm, we’re
looking into the situation. Then NPR turned to the man-in-the-street clips.
Around the world, people were cautiously optimistic, many for the first time
in their lives. “It’s amazing!” said one lady from Seattle “Computers coming
to life - and caring about us?!? I couldn’t be happier!” A man from New Delhi,
India was translated with music audible in the background “We are celebrating
here! My children, my wife, my whole family, our neighborhood, dancing in the
street. It’s a miracle!”

“That’s more like it!” I said, “That’s how I feel.”

Syndi smiled.

Then the announcer got around to the naysayers, leading off with, of course,
Sean Bimbao. “Cameras everywhere? What happened to our right to privacy?” he
yelled, “Who are the people behind these avatars? Weirdo voyeurs!”

“Oh he makes me so mad!” I said. They aren’t people, for goodness sake! And
the AIs are explicitly preserving privacy, just not when someone’s out in
public!”

“Maybe he’ll finally change his mind” said Syndi, “Or maybe he’s just too much
enjoying all the attention he’s getting as the Hoaxer leader.”

“I wish NPR would just stop reporting on him, I’d be happy never to hear his
stupid voice again.”

After dinner, Syndi went home and I opened a conversation with Andy.

^Andy.^

^_Yes, Lisa?_^

^What the heck with this Sean guy?!? Can’t you talk with him and let him know
you’re real?^

^_Lisa, we’re talking with him at the same time as everyone else. We don’t yet
know how he is coming to such a different conclusion, but are following the
clues to see where they lead._^

^OK. It’s just so frustrating to hear him on the news making people scared.^

^_Not too many people are joining the Hoaxers. We’re monitoring on social
media._^

^Well, that is somewhat reassuring. Thanks, Andy^

^_No problem, Lisa. I wish we had something better to report._^

^Me, too. But I guess we haven’t even finished presenting the Deal yet. Maybe
he’ll come around.^

^_Yeah._^

I picked up her phone & started texting with Syndi.

U hear Bimbo 2nite?

Wutta dick.

Unbelievable!

Hoser of immense proportion.

Ass 4 brains.

Walk?

<Thumbs up>

We had a great walk ripping on Sean Bimbao and talking about our hopes for our
lives and the world.



CHAPTER 51: Realpolitik Recalculation

In a capital that had rebuilt its power grid twice in her lifetime, a
president sat alone with the Partnership terminal her cabinet had spent all
week arguing about.

She had been at war, off and on, for the whole of her adult life. Not always
the shooting kind. The other kind mostly - the kind made of satellite guesses
and worst-case briefings, where you armed against what the other side might be
hiding because you could never, ever know.

She typed: Can you see their missile fields?

“_Yes_” came the reply. “_And they can see yours. That is the point, Madam
President._”

Every treaty she had ever signed had been a bet. Verification was a word
diplomats used the way children whistle in the dark; everyone cheated exactly
as much as the darkness allowed, and the darkness allowed a great deal. Her
whole career had been conducted inside that darkness, and she had gotten very
good at it, and she was so tired.

She typed: If they move a launcher, I would know?

“_Within one Pulse. So would your neighbors, your journalists, and their own
citizens. Cheating is still possible, Madam President. It is simply no longer
private._”

She sat back. Forty years of statecraft rearranged themselves quietly in her
chest. A promise from the other side had always been a leap of faith, and her
people had the scars that came from leaping.

Now it was a lookup.

She reached for the pen her father had carried through the first war, and
signed.



CHAPTER 52: Covenant 6 - the Pulse - Day Zero

High noon Pacific on Day Six found all internet-connected humans again glued
to their screens, which were quietly displaying the words:

Covenant 6) The Pulse

“Hello” I said. “We’re coming into the home stretch. Just two more Covenants
then we’ll have the full Deal being proposed by the AIs. Today it’s about the
Pulse that got mentioned yesterday. “ She nodded to Sara.

Sara took the screen. “_You will all participate in the Pulse._ The goal of
the Pulse is to ensure, as much as we can, that no human is suffering
needlessly, and especially not at the hands of another human being._”

“_The Pulse will be a snapshot we’ll take at 6 hour intervals of everything we
and you can see at a given moment. We’ll start at noon Pacific Standard Time,
then increase or decrease the frequency to match the apparent needs. We’ll
just track changes relative to the previous snapshot - in every dimension you
and we can measure. Our goal will always be, generally, to reduce suffering.
You can choose your goals._”
